- **in-silico-spectrum-metadata-curation**: Use when when processing collections of in-silico mass spectra from OMSLs (Open Mass Spectra Libraries) where the adduct field is absent, null, or not explicitly specified in the source file; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-spectrum-metadata-curation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
